- **task-executor**: Carries out a delegated run inside a task — edits, checks, a report back. The role the orchestrator hands its own plumbing to instead of doing it at three times the price. Not for stages that have their own role. ([totKtoXam/mustask](https://github.com/totKtoXam/mustask/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/task-executor.md))
- **task-executor**: Autonomous task execution engine — reads tasks from tasks.md, implements each in dependency order, runs verification, creates atomic commits, and tracks progress. The workhorse for batch implementation. - **tata-reviewer**: Revisa el cambio contra la spec aprobada y corre los tests. Usar en verify, en paralelo con tata-security. NO corrige código. ([NahuLemesF/tata-spec](https://github.com/NahuLemesF/tata-spec/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tata-reviewer.md))
- **tata-security**: Revisa la seguridad de un cambio o de una spec (auth, datos, entradas, dependencias, secretos). Usar en verify, en paralelo con tata-reviewer. NO corrige código. ([NahuLemesF/tata-spec](https://github.com/NahuLemesF/tata-spec/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tata-security.md))

Различен от Мобилджията и Хромаджията (строят продукта) и Правния Разбирач (право и GDPR). Никога не заобикаля ревюто. ([stefkostefko92-ux/Few-few](https://github.com/stefkostefko92-ux/Few-few/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tayniyat-agent.md))
